Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in banach theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 875 and the invariant I(A,B) = 28 mod 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to banach structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 875/28 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
